import "../../global.css";
import { QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Stack } from "expo-router";
import { StatusBar } from "expo-status-bar";
import { SafeAreaProvider } from "react-native-safe-area-context";

import { queryClient } from "#src/lib/query-client.ts";

const RootLayout = () => (
  <SafeAreaProvider>
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
      <Stack>
        <Stack.Screen name="index" options={{ title: "my-app" }} />
      </Stack>
      <StatusBar style="auto" />
    </QueryClientProvider>
  </SafeAreaProvider>
);

export default RootLayout;

#   tanstack-start-self-sqlite-expo-docker
#   tanstack-start-self-sqlite-expo-docker-e2e
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";

import { Text } from "#src/components/ui/text.tsx";
import { apiUrl } from "#src/env.ts";

const checkHealth = async () => {
  const response = await fetch(`${apiUrl}/api/health`);
  return response.ok;
};

export const ApiStatus = () => {
  const health = useQuery({ queryFn: checkHealth, queryKey: ["health"] });

  if (health.isPending) {
    return <Text className="text-muted-foreground">Checking the API…</Text>;
  }

  return health.data === true ? (
    <Text>API connected</Text>
  ) : (
    <Text className="text-destructive">API unreachable at {apiUrl}</Text>
  );
};

import Constants from "expo-constants";
import { z } from "zod";

import { resolveApiUrl } from "#src/lib/api-url.ts";

// Expo inlines EXPO_PUBLIC_* only where the code names the variable in full.
const configured = process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL;

export const apiUrl = resolveApiUrl({
  configured: z
    .url()
    .optional()
    .parse(configured === "" ? undefined : configured),
  dev: __DEV__,
  hostUri: Constants.expoConfig?.hostUri,
});

#   tanstack-start-self-sqlite-expo-docker
#   tanstack-start-self-sqlite-expo-docker-e2e
import { describe, expect, it } from "vite-plus/test";

import { resolveApiUrl } from "#src/lib/api-url.ts";

describe(resolveApiUrl, () => {
  it("prefers the configured origin", () => {
    expect(
      resolveApiUrl({
        configured: "https://api.example.com",
        dev: true,
        hostUri: "192.168.1.20:8081",
      })
    ).toBe("https://api.example.com");
  });

  it.each([
    ["192.168.1.20:8081", "http://192.168.1.20:3000"],
    ["10.0.2.2:8081", "http://10.0.2.2:3000"],
    ["localhost:8081", "http://localhost:3000"],
  ])("reaches the server beside Metro at %s in development", (hostUri, url) => {
    expect(resolveApiUrl({ configured: undefined, dev: true, hostUri })).toBe(
      url
    );
  });

  it("refuses a release build without an origin", () => {
    expect(() =>
      resolveApiUrl({
        configured: undefined,
        dev: false,
        hostUri: "192.168.1.20:8081",
      })
    ).toThrow("EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL");
  });
});

#   tanstack-start-self-sqlite-expo-docker-e2e
// Every app shape answers the API on :3000: Hono, or the framework that forwards to it.
const apiPort = 3000;

interface ApiUrlSource {
  readonly configured: string | undefined;
  readonly hostUri: string | undefined;
  readonly dev: boolean;
}

/** Development reaches the server on the machine Metro runs on; a release build needs EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL. */
export const resolveApiUrl = ({ configured, hostUri, dev }: ApiUrlSource) => {
  if (configured !== undefined) {
    return configured;
  }
  const host = hostUri?.split(":")[0];
  if (dev && host !== undefined && host !== "") {
    return `http://${host}:${apiPort}`;
  }
  throw new Error("Set EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL to the API origin for this build.");
};
